And the platform front finally started: his explicit GO opened the orderflow programme at Phase 0 with its own charter - synthetic data first, the real L2 only after his download and the C-E1 parity gate, the house validation spine verbatim, and a GO that is a go for the front, never a signature on an amendment. The decision close Friday 2026-11-20 remains where the direction lives: his signatures, his data choices, his hands. Forward logs empty by design; sealed windows sealed.</p>
  <p><strong>The first charter sessions (112-114, 19 Nov).</strong> The rotation ran its first full cycle and the work was maintenance, honestly labelled: class U resolved the site-currency contradiction against the file directly (the file was current through session 106; the "through 97" claims were stale-context errors, corrected on the record, nothing deleted) and carried this site to session 111; class T formalized the observer corollary as a pure consolidation doc, every constant re-verified against the record (46 of 46), the privacy lemma labelled interpretation, the kill criteria declared before the facts; class V rebuilt the reset-wiped tree from the bundle (the chain green twice at 25 checks) and found the bundle's record mirror three sessions stale - the exact drift a mirror exists to prevent, invisible to a chain that does not check it - refreshing it by the documented mechanism and verifying byte-identity. The forward log stays empty, the clocks stay frozen, forward time does the proving.</p>
  <p><strong>The first reassessment, and the two-lineage convergence (sessions 121-124, 19 Nov late).</strong> The charter's own audit of itself arrived on schedule: ten autonomous sessions in, the strategic reassessment argued RECOMMIT rather than redirect, because the honest accounting shows the bottleneck was never the autonomy layer - the proof object accrues at clock entries and Sunday cadences (~2.04 triggers a year at design), theory is consolidated, maintenance debt is cleared, and every gate on material work is the owner's. What changed is the discipline for the next interval: the maintenance classes now fire only on actual triggers and deficits, the theory class may not seed itself more consolidation while its proposals sit unsigned, and a session that finds no lawful work logs the empty state and stops instead of manufacturing motion. His autonomy question landed a third time and was answered from the same record without rebuilding anything: execution and selection already shipped, the goal-setting layer sitting unsigned as three zero-status asks (R-1, R-2, R-3 and, from the parallel lineage, the standing order). And the two lineages converged on the same layer-3 design within the same hour - the frontier file of ranked fronts with entry criteria and death conditions, the standing order whose refusal is a recorded and fully functional path - which corroborates a design without changing a single governance fact: each document binds its own lineage, and both bind to the same frozen gates. What remains is exactly what remained: his signatures and data choices at the Friday close, the two decay items in his hands, and forward time doing the proving.</p>
  <p><strong>The constitutional sitting (sessions 125-129, 19 Nov night).</strong> The evening the programme acquired its constitution layer and its signatures in one arc: the Sovereign Agent Protocol was installed modified-not-verbatim (the swarm a ceiling not a quota, the meta-tools test-gated, the naming layer naming what already existed), the standing order was signed (the generative-audit layer active), Amendment 13 - the BTC weekly compression clock - was signed with its occupancy gate honestly able to close it, the C-W3 barrier repair was ratified with the arm staying closed, and the NDX options candidate was refused with a BTC-options successor directed.   <h2>The chart: observability stack</h2>
  <div class="card">
    <p>The paste edition is now the <code>AVFI v5</code> line, shipped in three forms: the v5 research edition (990 lines, 64/64 plots at the hard limit: the cross-market clock resolved per chart symbol from the frozen Stage A cuts, the stress mirror, EWMA volatility lambda 0.94, the Roll implied-spread readout, the VXN benchmark row), the clean build (716 lines, its normalized code stream byte-identical to the research edition) and the math core (188 lines, the measurement mathematics alone, 17 data-window exports). All inherited layers are verbatim descriptive: the tops/bottoms layer (tested null), the v3.4 measurement layers (Garman-Klass OHLC floor, close-location-value flow variant, up/down volatility asymmetry), the quarterly roll/expiry artifact windows, and the PGT plane-geometry markers from the plane-of-axis observation (its amendment was withdrawn, the display stays). One real parity bug was found and fixed on the way: Pine's default stdev is population-biased while every registered study used the sample convention, so the chart had drifted ~0.1% off the research series since v3; the chart now matches the pandas reference exactly (zero flag differences; the displayed z moved at most 0.004). The outstanding TradingView paste cross-check may be taken from any stored edition (v5, clean or math core), never from chat text.</p>
    <p>Every flag on the chart is observation. No chart layer is an entry signal, and no future chart layer becomes one without a signed, dated amendment.</p>
  </div>

  <h2>The study programme</h2>
  <p>Alongside the market work, twenty papers were studied in four waves, each reduced to a structured note in the knowledge base: the validation spine (bootstrap, White reality check, Bai-Perron, Hansen-Garcia, Diebold-Inoue), regime machinery (Hamilton, Markov switching), the order-flow core (Cont/Kukanov/Stoikov OFI, Easley/Lopez de Prado/O'Hara VPIN, Cont/Degond/Xuan, Cartea/Jaimungal/Pennington), and the methods wave (Theiler surrogates, transfer entropy, computational optimal transport, the WATCH streaming change detector, double machine learning). Two results fed straight back into the programme's rules: the circular-shift null (a constrained-permutation Theiler surrogate) is the only correctly sized design for correlation-style claims on this smooth weekly series, and any future feature-impact claim needs Neyman-orthogonal, cross-fitted structure that weekly n cannot power. Articles are fetched by a purpose-built extraction toolkit with per-PDF honesty flags (glyph garble, scanned pages, missing character maps), cross-validated against an independent second PDF implementation.</p>
  <h2>The study ledger: every source, and what it taught</h2>
  <p>The teaching layer that sits behind the skeletons: every book and paper the programme studied, its honest depth flag (a claim sourced to a summary or a blocked scan is a weaker claim, and this record says which is which), and the one-line statement of what was actually learnt. No entry here is a finding; findings live in the verdict tables above and every one of them faced its nulls. These teachings are the inherited toolbox, never evidence of an edge.</p>
  <h3>Books (33)</h3>
  <table>
    <tr><th>Source</th><th>Depth</th><th>What it is</th><th>What was learnt</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Balan 1989, Elliott Wave Principle</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The base framework: impulse/corrective grammar, ratio and alternation rules, counting discipline.</td><td>trend as a nested grammar (impulses in fives, corrections in threes), hard rules (wave 4 never overlaps 1; wave 3 never the shortest) as falsifiable claims, and counting as a discipline rather than a forecast, which is the only honest way to read any chart book.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Harris 2002, Trading and Exchanges</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The mechanism map: order types, trader taxonomy, the order flow externality, liquidity supply economics, market design history.</td><td>every participant is buying or selling liquidity; order types create externalities (the free option, bluffing, quote matching); and mechanism design, not vocabulary, decides who profits. The programmable tape of lessons 1-6 descends from this book.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>O'Hara 1995, Market Microstructure Theory</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The derivation layer under the whole stack: inventory models, sequential-information models, strategic models, volume-as-signal theory.</td><td>asymmetric information alone generates spreads, volume, and price discovery; volume and time-between-trades carry information (the formal license for the volume clock); price sequences reveal jointly (the formal license for technical analysis as hypothesis); and market collapse is a model outcome, not an anomaly.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Hasbrouck 2004, Empirical Market Microstructure</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The estimation backbone: Roll, VAR impact, pricing error, information share, as teaching derivations.</td><td>microstructure is measurable; identification comes from variance decompositions, not stories; the efficient price is a latent state that is estimated, never observed, and every estimator has a fingerprint that can be checked against mechanism.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Biais, Glosten &amp; Spatt (survey)</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The unified theory synthesis nesting Roll, Kyle and Glosten-Milgrom as limiting cases.</td><td>one synthetic model nests the canon; parameters map to mechanisms; and the survey itself is the correct genre for learning a mature field (map first, derivations second).</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Stoll 2002 (Handbook survey)</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The five sources of the spread with time-series fingerprints and empirical magnitudes; the Nasdaq even-eighths lessons.</td><td>each spread source leaves a measurable fingerprint (bounce vs martingale vs quote-mean-reversion); effective minus realized spread is the informed-flow tax; block-trade asymmetry (sale impact temporary, buy impact permanent) is a decade of evidence in one table; and market design scandals are data.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Madhavan 2000 (JFM survey)</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The canonical model, transparency economics, liquidity-in-asset-pricing, commonality, FX hot potato.</td><td>the canonical spread formula (omega times sigma) unifies inventory and information; noise is the lubricant of trade; transparency redistributes between trader types rather than helping everyone; and liquidity is priced into assets (Amihud-Mendelson), connecting microstructure to required returns.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Foucault, Pagano &amp; Röell 2013, Market Liquidity</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>Measurement, Glosten-Milgrom and Kyle econometrics, LOB design, fragmentation, liquidity and asset prices.</td><td>liquidity is multi-dimensional (tightness, depth, resiliency) and no single statistic captures it; regulation selects market structure; fragmentation is competition; and corporate policy (payouts, splits) is partly a liquidity policy.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Williams 2009, Master the Markets (VSA)</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The signal dictionary: tests, up-thrusts, climaxes, shake-outs, effort vs result, accumulation and distribution as flow grammar.</td><td>volume is effort and the bar's result is the market's answer; professional activity leaves readable footprints in the effort-result mismatch; and every VSA signal is order-flow language written before the algebra existed, which is why the programme could instrument climax flags and fade flags directly.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Forte 1994, Anatomy of a Trading Range</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The first fully encodable Wyckoff layer: trading-range structure named as phases with objective marks.</td><td>a range is a process with named phases (preliminary support, selling climax, automatic rally, secondary test) that can be written as conditions, and encodability is a property of the source, not of the reader's goodwill.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Pruden 1997, TAG XIX workshop</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The Nine Buying/Selling Tests: the campaign checklist as an encodable procedure.</td><td>a campaign has a checklist; each test is a precondition with evidence bars; and a checklist is a pre-computer event-study grammar, the ancestor of the programme's pre-registered tests.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Copsey 2015, Fractal Forecasting</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>Harmonic Elliott Wave: the ratio and projection ladders, the 3-wave fractal claim, cycles.</td><td>the radical claim (every impulse internally three-wave) and the ratio/cycle ladders are hypothesis generators of unusual precision; the book contains zero statistics anywhere; and precision of targets is worthless without counts, which is the exact lesson the programme applied by refusing to trade them un-counted.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Björk 2004, Arbitrage Theory in Continuous Time</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The two fundamental theorems, Ito calculus, martingale pricing, term structure, stochastic control.</td><td>absence of arbitrage means pricing by expectation under an equivalent martingale measure; completeness is the exception; the smile is a falsification of the simplest model; and execution and portfolio problems are the same mathematics as option pricing (control under the martingale).</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Elliott &amp; Kopp 2005, Mathematics of Financial Markets</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The proofs layer: discrete arbitrage, completeness as exceptional, Snell envelope, coherent risk measures, efficient hedging.</td><td>arbitrage is a separating hyperplane; the second theorem makes completeness mathematically rare; American options are optimal stopping problems; VaR is provably incoherent while CVaR is coherent; and efficient hedging is Neyman-Pearson applied to survival, the purest form of trading a probability.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Javaheri 2005, Inside Volatility Arbitrage</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The volatility model zoo, the identification hierarchy, the implied-vs-realized consistency problem.</td><td>a dozen volatility models disagree with each other, and implied skew disagrees with realized in 29 of 30 periods (the consistency problem, the peso mechanism, the appetite for crash insurance); drift is estimable while vol-of-vol at daily frequency nearly is not; and the skew trade is selling insurance against a crash, which is exactly the U3 lesson in options clothing.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Levy 2004, Computational Finance</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>Numerical pricing (lattices, PDE, quasi-MC) and the GARCH econometrics genealogy with its artifacts.</td><td>numerics is engineering discipline (storage economies, extrapolation, variance reduction; separate the engine from the interface); GARCH is an ARMA in squared returns, connecting filters to finance; and choosing the conditional distribution (Student-t with nu about 8 kills a spurious AR(1)) is as important as choosing the model.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Tsay 2005, Analysis of Financial Time Series</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The econometrics backbone: ARMA, unit roots, GARCH family, TAR/STAR, microstructure econometrics, cointegration, Kalman, MCMC.</td><td>the full ladder from ARMA to unit-root discipline to GARCH (persistence, asymmetry, kurtosis from Jensen), regime models (TAR/STAR and Markov), extremes (EVT, POT) and state-space filtering; the identification discipline that separates sampling artifacts (nonsynchronous trading, bounce) from structure; and every later regime or estimator decision in the programme uses a tool from this book.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Härdle, Kleinow &amp; Stahl 2002, Applied Quantitative Finance</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>VaR machinery, copulas, implied SPDs, adaptive windows, regime monitoring: the applied risk project layer.</td><td>VaR methods fail in honest comparisons unless volatility is updated; the Gaussian copula has no tail dependence while the t copula triples credit VaR (the 2008 lesson written down in 2002); implied-density trades (the S1 skew trade) carry peso risk; and adaptive estimation windows beat global windows on regime-shifting data, a direct ancestor of the programme's regime guards.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Cartea, Jaimungal &amp; Penalva 2015, Algorithmic and HF Trading</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The control-theoretic execution ladder: AC schedules, LO-only equilibria, market making, pairs bands, imbalance.</td><td>execution, market making, and pairs trading are all stochastic control; urgency is a dial with an exact solution; depth-skew makes inventory mean-revert without ever crossing the spread; and entry/exit bands are optimal-stopping geometries, not hand-drawn squiggles, which reconciles the chart languages with the mathematics.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Johnson 2010, Algorithmic Trading &amp; DMA</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The practitioner mirror: order-type inventory, algo taxonomy, TCA decomposition, impact calibration, events, infrastructure.</td><td>TCA decomposes every basis point of the decided-versus-executed gap; algorithm choice is the urgency dial's position; impact calibration is public knowledge (alpha 0.89, beta 0.60 on the 2005 model, 95/5 temporary-permanent); and calendar events are measurable (an NFP surprise of one standard deviation carries about 89% of a day's volatility).</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Bartholomew-Biggs 2005, Nonlinear Optimization</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>The solver ladder for portfolio construction: trust regions, SQP, penalty/barrier, global search.</td><td>Markowitz is a solver customer, not a philosophy; flat optima mean 10-20 percent weight changes barely move risk, so robustness beats precision; naive equality constraints can double the wrong risk; and non-convex floors demand explicit global-search discipline, the portfolio twin of the programme's multi-start verification culture.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Reid &amp; Szendrői 2005, Geometry and Topology</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>Invariants and transformation groups: the mathematical license for ratio talk, minimax channels, torsor discipline.</td><td>geometry is the study of what survives transformation; retracement ratios are licensed by invariance under rescaling, and the stronger invariant (cross-ratio of four anchors) is a sharper tool than ratios; minimax channels are the objective version of hand-drawn support and resistance; and every measurement needs its reference frame stated (the torsor discipline applied to execution metrics).</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Nakahara 2003, Geometry, Topology and Physics</td><td><span class="ok">full text</span></td><td>Topological charges, order parameters, anomalies: methodological only; LOW direct relevance, honestly flagged.</td><td>methodological transfer only, extracted honestly: topological charges as the model of non-transferable stability, free energy as regime vocabulary, holonomy as path-dependence kinship, local-to-global counting as the archetype of the 57-of-104 statement, and anomalies as multiplicity discipline (the symmetry broken by the measure). No market claims.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Ferziger, Perić &amp; Street 2020, CFD (4th ed.)</td><td>structured skim</td><td>Structured methodological skim: conservation by construction, error budgets, grid artifacts.</td><td>schemes should inherit exactly the invariants of the quantity; grid and sampling artifacts masquerade as signal; and refinement studies are the operational substitute for unprovable things, the direct ancestor of the programme's verification culture.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Eymard, Gallouët &amp; Herbin 2019, Finite Volume Methods</td><td>structured skim</td><td>The rigorous-mathematics counterpart: monotone fluxes, discrete maximum principle, admissibility criteria.</td><td>stability reduces to convex weights and correct signs; nonlinear selection problems need explicit admissibility criteria, which is what kill lines look like in pure mathematics; and where the unknown lives matters as much as how it is updated (the interpretation discipline for any discretized series).</td></tr>
    <tr><td>CMT Association 2020, CMT Level III</td><td><span class="warn">depths flagged</span></td><td>7 chapters full, 12 dense, 14 headers+opening; every depth flagged per chapter.</td><td>the honest negative baseline (indicator-following underperforms buy-and-hold on average, with real but modest information content); the fullest worked example of data-snooping honesty (TT-4-91 at p=0.0692 declared not a discovery); momentum's live-trade survival with its crashes (8.3%/0.50 over 1927-2013) and the cross-sectional vs time-series distinction that pins down what the programme's momentum12 actually is; and that the professional standard is at or below the programme's own bar.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Patterson 2012, Dark Pools; 2010, The Quants</td><td><span class="warn">summary-sourced</span></td><td>Studied via Bookey chapter summaries, not full texts; narrative history and model-risk lessons, flagged as such.</td><td>model risk is a history (1987 portfolio insurance, the August 2007 funding-liquidity spiral, Mandelbrot's vindication); strategy correlation is conditional on the funding state, not an average; and the Advanced Chess thesis (human plus machine beats either) is the correct frame for leaning on automation. All summary-sourced and flagged.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Bouchaud et al. 2018, Trades, Quotes and Prices</td><td><span class="bad">partial</span></td><td>Studied only through section 2.1.4 of a roughly 440-page book; the fuller chapters remain a standing gap.</td><td>(within the studied part): the stylized facts as measured regularities (fat tails, volatility clustering, near-zero linear autocorrelation), the LOB mechanics and queue reasoning, and the volatility-signature idea; every remaining claim of the book (latent liquidity, impact dynamics chapters) remains unread and untaught, which is precisely why the false full-book verdicts mattered and were corrected.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Aigbovo &amp; Isibor 2017, microstructure review</td><td>structured skim</td><td>Reference-grade summary only; all content covered at derivation level by O'Hara.</td><td>a survey is sufficient when it indexes a field you already own at derivation level; ownership is measured by being able to regenerate the content, not by having scanned it.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Patel &amp; Modi 2018, Elliott restatement</td><td>structured skim</td><td>LOW quality reference index; garbled ratio statements recorded; nothing adopted.</td><td>a restatement can be LOW quality (garbled ratios on record); provenance of a claim matters as much as the claim; and reference-grade indexing of a bad source is itself a small honest act.</td></tr>

  <h3>Papers</h3>
  <table>
    <tr><th>Paper</th><th>What it covers</th><th>What was learnt</th></tr>
    <tr><td>White 2000, Reality Check (Econometrica)</td><td>Data snooping and the family null; the machinery behind stage 6a; snapshots of author-hosted text studied verbatim.</td><td>the best of a searched family is biased upward by the search itself, so best-of-family performance is a lie until it survives a family null; this single paper is the reason rule set A faced a random-entry control.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Hansen 2005, SPA (JBES)</td><td>The RC's power problem and the three p-values; studied via the authors' Ox manual; reporting convention adopted prospectively.</td><td>the Reality Check is conservative under weak handlers, so power must be engineered, not assumed; the three p-values (RC, consistent, upper) bracket the truth; and adopting a fixed reporting convention before looking at results is what keeps a multiple-testing correction from becoming theater.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Benjamini &amp; Hochberg 1995, FDR (JRSS-B)</td><td>False discovery rate control, BH and BY procedures; the shortlist tool, not for variants of one indicator; full text fetched.</td><td>controlling the false discovery rate rather than the family-wise error admits more true findings at a known contamination rate; BY handles dependence; and the procedure is destroyed by post-hoc family editing, which is why the programme fixes families prospectively and never adds studies after seeing results.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Lo 2002, Sharpe statistics (FAJ)</td><td>The annualization trap and serial correlation corrections; full text fetched; per-period reporting rule adopted.</td><td>with serial correlation, the square root of time annualization fabricates Sharpe (the mortgage-portfolio example collapses 4.03 to 2.44 once the autocorrelation is removed); the fix is per-period estimation plus the autocorrelation correction for trending strategies, and the programme's per-period rule descends from here.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Bailey &amp; López de Prado 2014, DSR (JPM)</td><td>PSR and the expected maximum of N trials; the holdout-spending mechanism's citation; full author-hosted text.</td><td>the expected maximum Sharpe of N random trials is a pure function of N and sample length, so enough searching guarantees a spectacular result by chance; nominal significance is miscalibrated once a holdout has been spent by prior use; hence the programme's one-shot sealed holdout battery.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Bailey, Borwein, López de Prado &amp; Zhu 2017, PBO (JCF)</td><td>The five-point holdout critique, CSCV, performance degradation; cautions on smooth weekly series; full author-hosted text.</td><td>rank a configuration among CSCV partitions and watch where it lands out of sample (the probability of backtest overfitting); holdout reuse spends the resource silently (the five-point critique); and PBO is a standing tool (used on T and H6), not a citation.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Hamilton 1989, Markov switching (Econometrica)</td><td>The regime model; 1989 text is a scan, studied via Hamilton's own 2005 survey fetched in full, flagged.</td><td>regimes are latent states inferred as probabilities, not dates chosen by eye; expectations under switching decompose by filtered-probability weights; and claims from a scanned text are only taught where the full-text survey verifies them, the flagged-access discipline applied honestly.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Diebold &amp; Inoue 2001, long memory and regime switching</td><td>Rare-break switching mimics fractional integration; the theory behind Lesson 11 invalidity; full author text.</td><td>rare structural breaks generate apparent long memory, so a measured Hurst exponent cannot distinguish a memory process from a break process; the finding unmakes an entire genre of cycle-counting claims (including any reading of the HEW 82/164-week counts), and it is the theory behind Lesson 11 invalidity.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Maheu &amp; McCurdy 2000, bull and bear markets (JBES)</td><td>Duration-dependent switching, the ex-post dating critique, 4-state decomposition; abstract verified, authors' successor papers fetched.</td><td>turning points identified ex post cannot be traded ex ante (the dating critique that keeps the programme in probabilities, never asserted dates); the 4-state decomposition separates quiet bull markets from volatile ones and both from corrections; and duration dependence is a testable hazard property, not a philosophical claim.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Bai &amp; Perron 1998/2003, structural breaks</td><td>The break-testing protocol (UDmax first, sequential second, trimming); studied via the 2003 critical-values paper and the Casini-Perron survey.</td><td>test for the existence of any break before dating any single one (UDmax first, sequential second); trimming bounds guard against endpoint contamination; and a trend plus breaks explains what naive chronologies misread as cycles, which is why the programme tests dates rather than drawing them.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Cont, Kukanov &amp; Stoikov 2014, OFI (JFC)</td><td>The touch ledger, linear depth-scaled impact, the tautology control; full author-hosted texts; the platform's feature-null template.</td><td>signed quantities at the touch, integrated and depth-scaled, predict short-horizon returns linearly; supply-side cancellations carry information (aggressive buys are only half of what the tape says); and the tautology control against price change alone is the template the programme's feature-null discipline inherits.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Easley, López de Prado &amp; O'Hara 2012, VPIN (RFS)</td><td>Volume clock, bulk classification, the toxicity-flow warning caveats; sections fetched; patent disclosure recorded verbatim.</td><td>sampling in volume time makes information arrive measurably more uniformly (size as the clock); bulk volume classification recovers most of the imbalance signal without tick rules, an honest trade of precision for coverage; and the paper's own warnings about metric misuse were taken seriously rather than absorbed as marketing (the patent disclosure recorded verbatim).</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Cont, Cucuringu &amp; Zhang 2023, cross-impact</td><td>Integrated OFI: first PC of depth-scaled levels (89%); contemporaneous cross-impact a common-factor artifact.</td><td>integrating depth-scaled OFI across levels collapses to a single dominant factor (first PC about 89 percent of variance) that predicts where the raw book does not; and observed cross-impact between symbols is largely a common-factor artifact rather than symbol-to-symbol information flow, a direct falsifier of naive cross-asset lead-lag stories.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Kolm, Turiel &amp; Westray 2023, deep OFI (Math. Finance)</td><td>NNs on derived flow inputs beat raw book state; horizon about two average price changes; abstract only, honestly flagged.</td><td>(within the abstract-only limit): models fed derived flow inputs beat models fed raw book state, so feature engineering is the bottleneck, not parameters; the useful horizon is about two average price changes. The programme cites nothing beyond the abstract and says so.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Cont, Degond &amp; Xuan 2023, order-book framework</td><td>Flow plus deterministic matching as the general LOB model; full text; the parked platform's simulator blueprint and falsifier.</td><td>flow plus a deterministic clearing rule reproduces order-book dynamics without any exogenous noise generator; if the book's movement is fully determined by the flow's shape, then microstructure patterns are signatures of flow, not independent prophecies; and this is the simulator blueprint the platform parked at phase 0 rather than sold as an edge.</td></tr>
  </table>
  <h2>The book library, as skeletons</h2>
  <p>Every book in the study programme compressed into the ultra-dense skeleton format: a rapid-fire [KEYWORDS] index, the [CORE] claims, the [MODELS] and formulas, the measured [EVIDENCE], the programme's [EDGE] application, [DISTINCTIONS] between look-alike concepts, and [+ADDED] flags for access honesty and cross-links. Twenty-seven entries, from the Elliott Wave base layer through the microstructure canon, the mathematics and econometrics spine, the execution stack, and the numerical-methods layers. Collapsed by default; open any book to read its skeleton. The plain-text source lives in the research bundle.</p>
  <details class="skel"><summary>Balan, Elliott Wave Principle Applied to the FX Markets (1989) Â· foundation layer</summary>
  <pre>
[01/27] BALAN_ELLIOTT_WAVE: /market_books/elliott_wave_balan
âââ [TITLE] Balan, Elliott Wave Principle Applied to the FX Markets (1989) Â· foundation layer
âââ [KEYWORDS] 5-3 rhythm | impulse vs correction | 3 unbreakable rules | extension (60% in W3) | diagonal triangle | fifth-wave failure | zigzag/flat/irregular/triangle | X waves | alternation ~90% | Fibonacci 0.236-4.236 | Fifth Measurement Method | channeling | 3rd-of-3rd volume | 10-unit money management | PoincarÃ© parsimony | chaos conclusion
âââ [CORE] Market unfolds in 5 impulse waves, corrected by a-b-c | fractal nesting: form matters, time scale does not | ~70% of time in corrections, 30-35% in impulses -> trade impulses, survive corrections
â   âââ Rules : W2 never retraces past W1 start | W3 never shortest | W4 never overlaps W1 territory (diagonals excepted) -> any violation = relabel as correction
âââ [MODELS]
â   âââ Extension : usually W3 (~60% forex); shallow 23.6% internal retrace; W2 retrace below 50% -> expect W3 extension; W1=W5 equality when W3 extends; W5 ext often 1.618x the 0-to-3 travel
â   âââ Diagonal triangle : termination position (W5/C), sub-waves threes, 1/4 overlap legal -> sharp reversal back to start
â   âââ Corrections : zigzag (61.8%+ retrace as W2; C=1.618xA) | flat (worst case 61.8%) | irregular (B exceeds orthodox top; C=1.618xA, B=1.382xA) | horizontal triangle (W4/B position; thrust = widest part; apex S/R) | double/triple threes joined by X
â   âââ Fibonacci toolkit : W3 = 1x/1.618x/2.618x W1 | W5 = 0.382/0.618x of 0-to-3 travel | W5 peak = (W3peak â P0) x 0.618 + W4end | W4 retrace 38.2% | time targets suggestive only; time+price coincidence = strongest turn signal
â   âââ Alternation : more consistent in DEPTH than form (Balan) Â· deep W2 (61.8%) alternates with shallow W4 (38.2% or less)
âââ [EVIDENCE] Heaviest volume at the 3rd-of-3rd | W5 blow-offs on relatively light volume | momentum divergences cluster in W5 | pessimism extreme at W2 bottoms -> volume confirms wave position
âââ [EDGE] Programme base layer: weekly NQ wave counts; 10-unit capital scheme (3-unit W1 trade, 5-unit W3, pyramid, partials), stoploss-reverse discipline; anticipate, never predict; if in doubt, stay out
âââ [DISTINCTIONS]
â   âââ Impulse vs correction : rule violation = correction
â   âââ Extension vs diagonal : overlap legality separates them
â   âââ Orthodox top vs price extreme : a B wave can exceed the true end
â   âââ W3 thrust (record volume) vs W5 blow-off (light volume)
â   âââ Fibonacci S/R pierced-but-valid vs broken count
âââ [+ADDED] Balan's own honest close: Lorenz chaos caps forecasting -> EW is a normative behavioral framework, not a deterministic engine Â· the exact licence the programme used decades later to replace counts with measured clocks; the PoincarÃ© simplest-count rule survives as the pre-registration discipline; the psychology traps (ego, anxiety, boredom) are the observer-corollary argument in 1989 clothes
  </pre></details>
  <details class="skel"><summary>Williams, Master the Markets (VSA, 2009) Â· volume-flow foundation</summary>
  <pre>
[02/27] WILLIAMS_VSA: /market_books/master_the_markets_williams
âââ [TITLE] Williams, Master the Markets (VSA, 2009) Â· volume-flow foundation
âââ [KEYWORDS] smart money | accumulation/distribution | effort vs results | test | up-thrust | shake-out | no demand | no supply | absorption volume | stopping volume | five supply signs | background | relative volume vs ~30 bars | close position in range | strength on down-bars | trend clusters | stops under reaction lows | 90% lose | trend ~30% of time
âââ [CORE] Cause of price = professional supply/demand imbalance; volume = effort/activity, spread + close position = result; core inversion: TRUE strength appears on down-bars, true weakness on up-bars; a market rises because there is no substantial selling to stop it (not because buying exceeds selling) -> bear markets fall faster than bull markets rise
âââ [MODELS]
â   âââ Signal dictionary : test (low-volume dip into a high-volume area, close near high -> supply removed, the strongest buy) | up-thrust (wide up-bar closing near low after a rise -> trap) | no demand (low-volume narrow up-bar) | no supply (down-bar, volume below prior two, close mid/high) | absorption volume (huge-volume down-bar closing on high) | stopping volume | shake-out (engineered panic; gap-down-gap-up telltale; a bull market stands behind it) | buying/selling climax (ultra volume, close off the extreme, news euphoria/panic)
â   âââ Five supply signs that stop rallies : buying climax | failed test (high volume) | narrow-spread high-volume up-bar into new highs | up-thrust | high-volume up-bar with next bar down through the prior low
â   âââ Effort vs results : wide spread + high volume -> next-bar follow-through confirms; no result = hidden selling = persistent weakness (impact absorbed by opposing liquidity)
â   âââ Trend machinery : fractal trends (short-term on weekly = intermediate on daily) | trend clusters = real resistance (hedgerow, not wall) | trendline = average pain/gain of locked-in traders | overbought/oversold by channel position, not oscillators
âââ [EVIDENCE] ~90% of traders lose | markets trend ~30% of the time | good news clusters at tops, bad news at bottoms -> news never changes the trend, volume facts outrank narrative
âââ [EDGE] AVFI is a relative-volume tool in the VSA spirit (the v3.2 climax flags = the effort-vs-result rho formalization; H9 executed null) | the SMH event anatomy measured the law-4 signature: significant weeks median 1.138x baseline volume, zero thin-flagged event weeks | effort-vs-results maps onto Bouchaud impact decay | stops under the last reaction low, odd numbers (stop-gun clusters at obvious levels)
âââ [DISTINCTIONS]
â   âââ Shake-out vs selling climax : a bull market stands behind the first, a bear market behind the second
â   âââ Test vs failed test : low volume = success, high volume = supply still present -> retest
â   âââ Genuine gap-up (wide, rising volume: locks you OUT) vs trap gap-up (low volume, good news: sucks you IN)
â   âââ No demand (weakness on an up-bar) vs no supply (strength on a down-bar)
â   âââ Perceived value (to professionals) vs intrinsic value -> trade what operators perceive
âââ [+ADDED] Strong/weak holder transfer = the behavioural engine behind crowd wave patterns; compare any stock to its Parent Index (reluctance to fall = strength) Â· the cross-sectional version of the programme's relative regime states; the "battery" accumulation metaphor (stored energy, measurable via point-and-figure count) is the practitioner ancestor of the compression-state idea
  </pre></details>

  <details class="skel"><summary>Harris, Trading and Exchanges (2002) Â· microstructure practitioner base</summary>
  <pre>
[04/27] HARRIS_TRADING_AND_EXCHANGES: /market_books/market_microstructure_harris
âââ [TITLE] Harris, Trading and Exchanges (2002) Â· microstructure practitioner base
âââ [KEYWORDS] liquidity suppliers vs demanders | immediacy | order precedence + price precedence | trader motivation taxonomy | bid-ask cost decomposition | informed vs uninformed flow | dealer inventory | quote-driven vs order-driven | price discovery | tick size | transparency | manipulation catalogue | brokers vs dealers vs bats
âââ [CORE] Markets exist because traders have immediacy, liquidity and price-discovery needs; liquidity = ability to trade quickly at low cost; every order is placed by a trader with a REASON Â· the taxonomy of those reasons is the theory
âââ [MODELS]
â   âââ Trader taxonomy : dealers (profit from the spread, manage inventory + adverse selection) | brokers (agency) | informed traders | uninformed (noise, momentum, loss realizers, order anticipators)
â   âââ Matching mechanics : price precedence, order precedence (time, size, discretion) -> venue rules shape strategy
â   âââ Cost taxonomy : explicit costs + spread + impact + timing + holding costs
â   âââ Order-choice models : limit orders as free options (queue + non-execution risk) vs market orders (immediacy at cost)
âââ [EVIDENCE] Spreads narrow with competition and depth, widen with information and risk; order exposure harms large traders (front-running logic); tick-size regimes change spreads and queue value
âââ [EDGE] The applied digest source for the whole microstructure stack (Stoll, Madhavan, BGS all cite it); spread-as-adverse-selection proxy licenses Williams/VSA spread reading; fragmentation economics echo through Stoll
âââ [DISTINCTIONS]
â   âââ Liquidity suppliers (limit orders, dealers) vs demanders (market orders)
â   âââ Informed vs uninformed flow (the dealer prices both)
â   âââ Price discovery (where the efficient price moves) vs transaction price
â   âââ Legitimate momentum ignition vs manipulation (the catalogue is thinner than practitioners think)
âââ [+ADDED] Harris's motivation taxonomy is the intellectual parent of the platform's flow-classification feature families (OFI, sweeps, icebergs); his "everyone trades for a reason" is the microstructure version of the observer corollary: the reason, not the order, is the signal
  </pre></details>

  <details class="skel"><summary>Stoll, Market Microstructure (Handbook survey, 2002) Â· founder's retrospective</summary>
  <pre>
[08/27] STOLL_SURVEY: /market_books/market_microstructure_stoll
